Structure 106.1src/New/Lift/CupProductBound.lean: Lift.KummerChart.PairingSide
Let $p \in \Natural$. Let $O \in \LiftKummerChartCoeffsMACRoBWI$. Then $\LiftKummerChartPairingSideMACRoBYK {O}$ is a set.
One side of a pairing: a lattice datum Y (`Lift.KummerChart.JumpLattice`) with 0 in every jump set U_i, a lattice C, and an O-linear map α: C → Z_f(Y), the crystalline cocycles.

Definition 106.69src/New/Lift/CokernelKillers.lean: Lift.IsInJumpWindow
Let $\eta , b , W \in \Natural$. Let $\epsilon \in \UnitsMACRoEL {\Integer}$. Let $U$ be a finite set of $\Integer$. Then $\LiftIsInJumpWindowMACRoBWF {\eta}{\epsilon}{b}{W}{U}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all elements $u$ of $\Integer$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {U}{u}$, then both $\NegnegMACRoFV {\eta}\leq u$ and $u \leq \eta$, $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\epsilon}= 1$, $b \leq u$ and $u \leq b + W$ or $\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {\epsilon}= \NegnegMACRoFV {1}$, $b \leq \NegnegMACRoFV {u}$ and $\NegnegMACRoFV {u}\leq b + W$.
The condition that the finite set U of integers lies in [−η, η] ∪ ε·[b, b + W], the union of a low and a far window, for a sign ε.
Definition 106.70src/New/Lift/CokernelKillers.lean: Lift.raisedAtE

Definition 106.71src/New/Lift/CokernelKillers.lean: Lift.twoWindows
Let $n \in \Natural$. Let $i$ be an integer. Then $\LifttwoWindowsMACRoCBE {n}{i}$ is a finite set of $\Integer$ defined as the union of $\FinsetIccMACRoEE {\NegnegMACRoFV {n}}{n}$ and $\FinsetIccMACRoEE {(i - n)}{(i + n)}$.
The finite set of integers [−n, n] ∪ [i − n, i + n], two windows of radius n centred at 0 and at i.
Definition 106.72src/New/Lift/CokernelKillers.lean: Lift.NoMultipleNear
Let $p , n \in \Natural$. Let $b$ be an integer. Then $\LiftNoMultipleNearMACRoBZX {p}{n}{b}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all integers $y$, if $y$ is an element of the union of $\LifttwoWindowsMACRoCBE {n}{b}$ and $\LifttwoWindowsMACRoCBE {n}{\NegnegMACRoFV {b}}$, then if $p$ divides $y$, then $y = 0$.
The prime-to-p gap condition: p divides no nonzero integer within distance n of 0, b or −b.
